Result: 1) 婴儿宇宙场满足线性Wheeler-DeWitt方程，其模式系数可对角化，对应高斯α-区段路径积分；2) 盘状拓扑相干位移无边界态，诱导Hartle-Hawking波函数作为单点函数；3) 非平凡拓扑（如裤子图）导致非高斯α-区段测度；4) 在Marolf-Maxfield模型中，盘+圆柱截断可精确提升为包含所有拓扑的Fock空间表示。

Conclusion: 婴儿宇宙场论提供了一个自洽的、基于约束与群平均的多宇宙量子场论框架，其核心是线性场动力学与α-区段统计之间的张力——拓扑相互作用不改变场方程形式，而仅修正测度，暗示深层的‘拓扑重整化群’结构。

Abstract: Gravitational sums over spacetime topology suggest a baby-universe field theory whose quanta are entire universes. We develop such a description from group-averaged amplitudes that impose gravitational constraints and define physical inner products in the single-universe theory. To sharpen the contrast with ordinary QFT, we start with gravity on one-dimensional worldlines, which can arise from reducing higher-dimensional cylinders. For multiple noninteracting worldlines that pair boundary components, the baby-universe Hilbert space is the symmetric Fock space over the physical one-universe Hilbert space, and the universe field expands in the on-shell modes spanning the latter. Each mode multiplies the sum of creation and annihilation operators, so the mode coefficients commute. The mode expansion thus makes explicit the field's commutativity and its Wheeler-DeWitt linear field equation. Diagonalizing the coefficients recovers a statistical description by a Gaussian path integral over on-shell field configurations labelling superselection ($α$-)sectors.
  We take exploratory steps toward more general topologies. Disk-like topologies coherently shift the no-boundary state, turning on the Hartle-Hawking wavefunction as a one-point function. Even with nontrivial topologies represented by interacting Feynman diagrams, we argue that group averaging near each boundary still enforces the same linear field equation, while interactions instead enter through a non-Gaussian measure over $α$-sectors. In the topological Marolf-Maxfield model, we promote a disk-and-cylinder truncation to an exact Fock-space representation of the full theory incorporating all topologies. We also relate the truncated and exact inner products by an insertion of interactions, whose perturbative expansion produces a sum over time evolutions generated by pairs of pants, evoking an analogy to group averaging.

</details>


### [99] [An attempt to derive the LVS from a 10d EFT perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16117)
*Arthur Hebecker,Andreas Schachner,Gaetano Maria Sifo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在通量紧致化中非微扰标量势的10维直接推导问题，特别是LVS AdS势的体积压制因子与4维超引力预期之间的不一致，指出当前10维有效场论框架下难以解释该差异，可能需强对数修正但其起源不明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是直接从10维有效场论（EFT）出发推导大体积场景（LVS）的AdS标量势，以验证并理解其体积压制结构（如1/V、1/V²、1/V³项）的物理起源，而非依赖4维超引力和大体积展开。

Method: 通过分析10维EFT中的瞬子-反瞬子效应、单瞬子与3形式通量耦合、Weyl重标度及通量稀释机制，系统比较各项在10维与4维框架下的体积压制行为，并考察胶子凝聚情形以检验逻辑一致性。

Result: 发现10维EFT预测的前两项（exp(−2aτₛ)/V 和 W₀exp(−aτₛ)/V²）的总体体积压制强于4维结果（分别多出1/√V和1/V因子），存在无法用现有机制解释的不匹配；可能的对数Kähler修正需达O(1)量级，但其物理来源未知。

Conclusion: 当前10维EFT框架尚不能自洽重现LVS AdS势的精确体积依赖关系，揭示了低能有效理论衔接中的深层概念挑战，亟需更精细的10维紫外完成或新型修正机制。

Abstract: Non-perturbative scalar potentials are a central ingredient for stabilising moduli in flux compactifications. A key target in this context is the direct 10d derivation of the LVS AdS potential, i.e., the scalar potential of the Large Volume Scenario before the uplift. The familiar result, relying on 4d supergravity machinery and large volume expansion, consists of three terms of parametric form $\exp(-2aτ_s)/{\mathcal V}$, $W_0\exp(-aτ_s)/{\mathcal V}^2$, and $W_0^2/{\mathcal V}^3$. In plain 10d EFT language, the first term is an instanton-anti-instanton effect while the second term comes from a single instanton coupled to 3-form flux. When attempting to derive the suppression by the volume ${\mathcal V}$ directly from the 10d EFT, one encounters a puzzle: One expects a flux-dilution factor $1/\sqrt{\mathcal V}$ for the second term and a factor $1/{\mathcal V}^2$ from Weyl rescaling to the 4d Einstein frame for both the first and second term. Thus, the total volume suppression for these two terms is stronger than in the aforementioned 4d EFT expectations. We discuss the underlying logic in detail, also in the gaugino condensation case, but fail to find a satisfactory explanation for the mismatch. While log-corrections to the Kähler potential may be responsible, these would have to be strong enough to affect the outcome of the LVS scheme at the ${\mathcal O}(1)$ level. What is worse, the origin of the required correction remains unclear.

</details>


### [100] [Single-Instance Observables in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16130)
*Brian Swingle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对SYK模型中低权重Majorana字符串热期望值的图解展开方法，该方法在固定耦合实现下以N^{-1/2}为小参数展开，每阶可高效经典计算，并成功应用于四体SYK和Maldacena-Qi模型，提升了热场双重态保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算有限N SYK模型中热力学量和非平衡动力学仍具挑战，尤其在中等系统尺寸下；需发展一种既保持非微扰特征又可高效数值实现的解析方法。

Method: 基于大N melonic传播子构造温度依赖核函数，对低权重Majorana字符串热期望值进行图解展开，按N^{-1/2}逐阶组织，每阶为耦合常数的多项式与核函数乘积；扩展至任意虚时及短实时关联函数。

Result: 在N≤24的四体SYK模型中，图解展开结果与精确对角化高度吻合；在Maldacena-Qi模型中，识别出一个邻近SYK型哈密顿量，其热场双重态对MQ基态的保真度优于原始SYK哈密顿量的热场双重态。

Conclusion: 该图解展开提供了一种连接大N解析结构与有限N数值结果的有效桥梁，不仅验证了SYK模型的普适性，还为构造高保真热场双重近似及探索全息对偶提供了新工具。

Abstract: We present a diagrammatic expansion for thermal expectation values of low-weight Majorana strings in a fixed realization of the $N$-fermion Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) model. At each order in the expansion, the observable is expressed as a finite sum of polynomials in the couplings multiplied by temperature-dependent kernels built from the large-$N$ melonic propagator. At fixed $βJ$, the expansion is organized in powers of $N^{-1/2}$, and each order can be efficiently evaluated with classical resources polynomial in $N$. The expansion also extends to correlations at arbitrary imaginary times and sufficiently short real times. For the four-body SYK model, we show that the diagrammatic predictions give an excellent account of exact diagonalization results at sizes up to $N=24$. We also apply the method to the Maldacena--Qi (MQ) model formed from two identical copies of a fixed SYK realization. This enables the identification of a nearby SYK-like Hamiltonian whose thermofield double state has higher fidelity with the Maldacena--Qi ground state than the thermofield double of the original SYK Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [101] [$D$-term and $F$-term potential of non-Abelian D7-branes in IIB Calabi-Yau orientifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16136)
*Daniel Junghans*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过降维非阿贝尔DBI作用量，首次从第一性原理推导出IIB型弦理论Calabi-Yau定向流形紧化中D7-膜堆叠产生的4维D项和F项势能，并讨论了KK模积分掉后的修正及其对Calabi-Yau体积与膜场的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D7-膜在IIB型弦理论中的势能在模稳定（如T-膜上提的dS场景）中起关键作用，但此前D项和F项势能多基于不同论证分别推得，缺乏统一的第一性原理推导。

Method: 通过对D7-膜的非阿贝尔DBI作用量进行四维紧化降维，并纳入世界体积与ISD体通量效应及膜场的非阿贝尔性质，系统导出4维有效势能；同时分析KK模式积除带来的修正及其可控性条件。

Result: 给出了含通量与非阿贝尔结构的完整D/F项势能表达式，推广并统一了先前结果；明确了KK修正可控性对Calabi-Yau体积与膜场强度的约束；揭示了体通量对8维世界体积理论中BPS方程与势能的修正。

Conclusion: 该工作为IIB模稳定机制（尤其是T-膜dS构造）提供了更坚实、自洽的微观基础，并指出实验可控性需满足特定几何与场强条件。

Abstract: We derive the 4d $D$-term and $F$-term potential generated by a D7-brane stack in Calabi-Yau orientifold compactifications of type IIB string theory, taking into account the effect of worldvolume and ISD bulk fluxes and the non-Abelian nature of the brane fields. This potential is an important ingredient for moduli stabilization in IIB, e.g., in de Sitter scenarios using a T-brane uplift. While aspects of the $D$-term and $F$-term parts of the potential have been inferred before with various arguments, we provide here a unified first-principles derivation of the full potential by dimensionally reducing the non-Abelian DBI action of the D7-branes. Our result generalizes previous expressions and agrees with them where applicable, thus confirming the mutual consistency of the various approaches. Moreover, we provide a thorough discussion of corrections from integrating out KK modes and argue that demanding control over such corrections implies bounds on the Calabi-Yau volume and the brane fields. Aside from these various results for the 4d effective action, our calculation also shows how bulk fluxes correct the potential and the BPS equations in the 8d worldvolume theory that governs the microscopic dynamics of non-Abelian branes.

</details>


### [102] [Symmetry Descent in M-theory, Part I: A Twelve-Dimensional Parent Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16141)
*Pinak Banerjee,Subham Roy,Xingyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种M理论构型量子场论的对称性下降方法，从一个带三次拓扑相互作用的高形式BF理论出发，构建了具有广义Maxwell-Chern-Simons边缘动力学的规范不变体-边系统，并将11维超引力C场的局域通量方程（含I8引力修正）追溯至一个12维拓扑模型；进一步借助Hypothesis H，将体部方程与S^4的Sullivan模型关联，提出4-上同伦的扭曲量子化及体-边耦合场空间的同伦拉回描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解M理论中对称性如何从高维拓扑结构下降到低维物理动力学，特别是11维超引力C场的非线性通量方程的起源。

Method: 基于高形式BF理论与三次体部拓扑相互作用构造规范不变的体-边系统；引入度规依赖的边界作用量导出边缘的Maxwell-Chern-Simons动力学；利用Hypothesis H将体部方程对应于S^4的Sullivan模型，并采用扭曲4-上同伦量子化与同伦拉回描述场空间。

Result: 成功将11维超引力C场的局域通量方程（含I8修正）还原为12维拓扑模型的边界条件；揭示电-磁配对与非线性Maxwell-Chern-Simons动力学同源于单一高维拓扑结构；给出M理论场的局部动力学机制与全局拓扑刻画。

Conclusion: M理论的对称性下降可由一个12维拓扑模型统一实现；该框架不仅提供局域动力学解释，还通过上同伦与同伦论工具为M理论场提供了候选的全局数学表征。

Abstract: We initiate a symmetry descent procedure for M-theory engineered quantum field theories. Starting from a higher form BF theory supplemented by a cubic bulk topological interaction, we construct a gauge-invariant bulk-boundary system whose edge modes acquire generalized Maxwell--Chern--Simons dynamics after the introduction of a metric-dependent boundary action. The nonlinear contribution to the boundary equation is induced entirely by the cubic bulk interaction. In the case of twelve dimensional bulk parent, the resulting boundary conditions reproduce the local flux equations of the eleven-dimensional supergravity $C$-field, including the gravitational $I_8$ correction. Thus, the electric--magnetic pairing and the nonlinear Maxwell--Chern--Simons dynamics descend from a single 12D topological model. Using Hypothesis H, we identify the bulk equations with the Sullivan model of $S^4$ and interpret their nonlinear gauge transformations as homotopies. We then propose a twisted 4-cohomotopy quantization of the bulk fields and a homotopy pullback description of the coupled bulk--boundary field space. The construction provides both a local dynamical mechanism for M-theory symmetry descent and a candidate global characterization of its fields.

</details>


### [103] [Separation of variables for non-diagonalisable models: XXX with twisted boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16146)
*Juan Miguel Nieto García*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有缺陷扭曲矩阵的Heisenberg XXX自旋链，在量子分离变量框架下导出了刻画广义本征矢的'Jordanian TQ方程'，并发现高阶广义本征矢的分离基波函数需用弱组合求和表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有缺陷边界条件的可积自旋链中广义本征矢的结构与波函数特性，拓展传统TQ方程在非对角化情形下的适用性。

Method: 采用量子分离变量法（SoV），分析扭曲周期边界条件下XXX自旋链的谱问题，推导满足广义本征值问题的修正TQ型方程，并计算分离基下的波函数表达式。

Result: 建立了'Jordanian TQ方程'以刻画广义本征矢；证明真本征矢波函数可分离，而高阶广义本征矢波函数需表为弱组合的和。

Conclusion: 缺陷扭曲导致系统出现非对角Jordan块结构，传统可分性失效，需引入新的TQ型方程及组合结构描述广义本征态。

Abstract: We study the Heisenberg XXX spin chain with twisted periodic boundary conditions with a defective twist matrix. Using quantum separation of variables, we show that the generalised eigenvectors of this system can be characterised in terms of a modified TQ equation that we have named "Jordanian TQ equation". We also show that the wavefunction in the separated basis is only separable for true eigenvectors, while the wavefunction for generalised eigenvectors of higher rank is given instead by a sum over weak compositions.

</details>


### [104] [The Role of Completeness in Probing Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16153)
*Yuya Kusuki,Hiroyasu Tajima,Shion Yamashika*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在多体系统中对称性破缺的完整度量的重要性，提出了基于保真度的对数特征函数（LCF）扩展方法，并证明其相较于纠缠不对称性（EA）在区分动力学行为方面具有两大优势：一是LCF可保持广延性并反映初态依赖性，从而识别离散对称性Mpemba效应；二是不同LCF分量可呈现独特弛豫与交叉行为，而EA可能无法体现。研究涵盖自旋链淬火、单量子比特退相干及共形场论中的解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对称性破缺度量（如纠缠不对称性EA）在多体动力学中存在信息缺失问题，尤其难以分辨不同初态的弛豫行为和识别Mpemba效应；需一个更完备、物理意义更清晰的度量工具。

Method: 引入基于保真度的对数特征函数（LCF）混合态推广；对比分析LCF与EA在有限群对称性下的有界性、广延性及弛豫行为；通过自旋链淬火与单量子比特退相干模型进行数值与解析验证；构建量子场论中的复本方法并推导共形场论下的解析表达式。

Result: 1) LCF在热力学极限下仍具广延性且系数依赖初态与时间，能区分EA无法分辨的弛豫曲线并识别离散对称性Mpemba效应；2) 不同LCF分量呈现独特弛豫与交叉现象，部分情形下EA无交叉而LCF有；3) 在CFT中获得LCF的解析结果，并建立其复本构造。

Conclusion: LCF构成比EA更完备、更具物理分辨力的对称性破缺度量，其完整性不仅具信息论意义，更对理解多体对称性恢复动力学至关重要；该框架可推广至更广泛的量子场论与非平衡问题。

Abstract: Completeness is widely recognized in quantum information as an important property of a family of monotones, because it ensures that no information relevant to state conversion is lost. We show that completeness is also physically important in many-body systems: an incomplete measure of symmetry breaking can miss essential features of symmetry-restoration dynamics. We study the logarithmic characteristic function (LCF), also known as the string order parameter, whose full family is complete for exact i.i.d. pure-state conversion under symmetry-preserving operations for finite groups. We introduce a fidelity-based extension of the LCF to mixed states and identify two concrete advantages over entanglement asymmetry (EA). First, for a finite symmetry group $G$, EA is bounded by $\log |G|$ and can approach the same saturated value for different initial states in the thermodynamic limit, making their relaxation curves indistinguishable and preventing the identification of a discrete-symmetry Mpemba effect. By contrast, the LCF can remain extensive, with a coefficient that depends on the initial state and time, and therefore continues to distinguish their relaxation dynamics. Second, different LCF components can exhibit distinct relaxation and crossing behavior, including cases in which EA shows no crossing. We demonstrate these advantages in spin-chain quenches and a single-qubit system under depolarizing noise. We also develop a replica construction for the fidelity-based LCF in quantum field theory and derive analytical results for conformal field theory.

</details>


### [105] [Making the Great Desert Bloom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16154)
*Jacques Distler,Andreas Karch*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出，在‘大沙漠’假设下（即弱尺度到普朗克尺度间无新物理），标准模型可能具有一个半经典反德西特（AdS₄）真空，并存在一个三维共形场论（CFT₃）对偶；该情形还暗示至少存在一个远大于普朗克长度的额外维度，并给出一个可能的候选CFT₃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决‘大沙漠’这一看似贫瘠、缺乏新物理的场景是否仍蕴含深刻理论含义的问题，挑战其作为粒子物理‘噩梦情景’的传统看法。

Method: 基于量子引力与全息原理（AdS/CFT对应）进行理论推演，结合标准模型有效场论、真空稳定性及尺度分离等合理假设，分析大沙漠假设下的紫外完备性要求。

Result: 推导出在大沙漠假设下，标准模型必须具有半经典AdS₄真空及其CFT₃对偶，并要求存在显著大于普朗克长度的额外维度；提出一个具体的CFT₃候选者。

Conclusion: 大沙漠并非理论死胡同，反而可导出强约束性的量子引力预言，如AdS/CFT对偶与大尺度额外维度，为连接粒子物理与量子引力提供了新路径。

Abstract: The absence of new physics beyond neutrino masses between the weak scale and the Planck scale is referred to as the great desert. It is often seen as a nightmare scenario for particle physics. Here we argue, by contrast, that the great desert assumption in fact has dramatic implications: it predicts that the Standard Model has a semi-classical AdS$_4$ vacuum with CFT$_3$ dual and, under reasonable assumptions, must be accompanied by at least one extra dimension significantly larger than Planck length. We also present one plausible candidate CFT$_3$ to be ``the" dual to the Standard Model.

</details>


### [106] [Persistent Spontaneous Time-Reversal Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16162)
*Michael Smolkin,Lev Yung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个在2+1维中、局域、幺正、紫外完备的量子场论，通过相关Yukawa形变实现零温下对称性保持、有限温度下自发破缺宇称和时间反演的机制，并且破缺相在高温极限下依然稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一个在有限温度下能自发破缺离散对称性（如宇称P和时间反演T）且不被热效应恢复的、严格定义的量子场论模型，以理解非平衡或热致对称性破缺的新机制。

Method: 基于临界双锥矢量模型与自由无质量狄拉克费米子的直积，引入一个相关的Yukawa形变，研究其重整化群流；分析零温和有限温度下的真空结构与对称性实现。

Result: 发现该模型在零温下真空保持P和T对称性；当温度升高至某一临界值时发生‘逆热相变’，生成一个P、T奇的费米子质量，且该破缺相在任意高温下均稳定存在。

Conclusion: 首次在局域、幺正、紫外完备的2+1维QFT中实现了无热恢复的自发P/T破缺，为热致拓扑相变与手征对称性破缺提供了新范式。

Abstract: We provide an analytic realization of spontaneous parity and time-reversal breaking without thermal restoration in a local, unitary, ultraviolet-complete quantum field theory in $2+1$ dimensions with finitely many fields. The theory is defined by a relevant Yukawa deformation of a product of the critical biconical vector model and free massless Dirac fermions. This deformation induces a renormalization group flow whose infrared endpoint is a decoupled product of the critical vector model and the conformal Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model. At zero temperature, the vacuum preserves both symmetries, whereas upon heating, the theory undergoes an inverse thermal transition at a finite critical temperature. Above this temperature, a fermion mass, odd under parity and time reversal, is dynamically generated, and the broken phase persists to arbitrarily high temperatures.

</details>


### [107] [Schwinger-Keldysh effective actions for non-hydrodynamic poles and branch cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16164)
*Andrea Amoretti,Matteo Anselmi,Daniel K. Brattan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了具有分支切割的高斯Schwinger-Keldysh有效作用量，通过弛豫极点连续谱和弛豫时间密度的Stieltjes变换解释非流体力学行为，并用动力学KMS对称性、正定性和稳定性约束其谱性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解延迟热关联子在复频域中的非流体力学极点与分支切割如何限制流体力学的有效性。

Method: 构建高斯Schwinger-Keldysh有效作用量，引入弛豫时间密度及其Stieltjes变换；利用动力学KMS对称性、正定性与稳定性分析谱结构；结合零动量下动能理论中的能量依赖弛豫时间近似进行微观实现。

Result: 揭示了分支切割源于弛豫极点连续谱；端点行为决定局部分支结构与近边缘极点标度；均匀与幂律弛豫时间密度分别导致超所有阶极点-端点分离及极点-极点与极点-分支点终止的连续插值。

Conclusion: 该框架为非流体力学动力学提供了统一有效描述，连接了有效场论与微观动能理论，并阐明了分支切割的物理起源与约束条件。

Abstract: Retarded thermal correlators, analytically continued to complex frequency, exhibit non-hydrodynamic poles and branch cuts that constrain hydrodynamics. We construct Gaussian Schwinger-Keldysh effective actions in which branch cuts arise from continua of relaxation poles and response functions are governed by Stieltjes transforms of relaxation-time densities. Dynamical KMS symmetry, positivity and stability constrain the spectra, while endpoint behaviour controls the local branch structure and near-edge pole scaling. The illustrative examples of uniform and power-law densities exhibit respectively beyond-all-orders pole-endpoint separation and a continuous interpolation between pole-pole and pole-branch-point termination. Finally, we show that an energy-dependent relaxation-time approximation in kinetic theory provides a microscopic realization of our branch cuts at zero momentum.

</details>


### [108] [Instanton Interactions and the Axion Weak Gravity Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16165)
*Jarod Hattab,Eran Palti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于长程瞬子相互作用的排斥性轴子弱引力猜想（Repulsive Interaction Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture），该猜想以无质量标量场主导的瞬子作用为依据，比传统轴子弱引力猜想更强，并在无质量标量场时可能修正轴子衰变常数的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受弱引力猜想中带电粒子长程排斥相互作用启发，作者试图为轴子类场建立类似物理图像，聚焦于瞬子间的长程相互作用，尤其是由无质量标量场主导的部分。

Method: 通过分析欧几里得瞬子在大分离距离下的相互作用势，提取其主导项（纯由无质量标量场介导），忽略引力和规范场等次主导贡献，进而推导出对瞬子作用量中标量场依赖性的约束条件。

Result: 提出了排斥性轴子弱引力猜想，形式化为对复欧几里得瞬子作用量中标量场依赖性的新界；证明该界在标准紫外完备模型中蕴含并强化了传统轴子弱引力猜想；指出若不存在无质量标量场，传统轴子衰变常数上界可能不再适用。

Conclusion: 轴子弱引力猜想可被更基本地重构为仅涉及无质量标量场的排斥性相互作用约束，这不仅统一了物理图像，还揭示了其对低能有效理论结构（如是否存在无质量标量）的敏感依赖性。

Abstract: The Weak Gravity Conjecture admits a formulation in terms of long-range charged particle self-interactions being repulsive. We adopt a similar approach to the Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture by considering long-range instanton interactions. At leading order, in the instanton separation, such interactions are mediated purely by massless scalar fields. Gravitational and gauge interactions are sub-leading. We formulate a repulsive interaction Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture, which forms a bound on the scalar field dependence of the complex Euclidean instanton action. We show that in standard ultraviolet complete examples, this bound implies the usual formulation of the Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture, while generally being stronger. Because this formulation is purely a statement about massless scalar fields, it implies that in the absence of massless scalars the usual bound on the axion decay constant could be modified.

</details>


### [109] [A $dS_4$ maximum in M-theory from Casimir energies on Riemann-flat manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16169)
*Miquel Aparici,Bruno Valeixo Bento,Miguel Montero*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个来自M理论紧化于Riemann平坦流形 $T^7/\mathbb{Z}_8$ 的四维显式de Sitter鞍点解，是首个自上而下实现的四维dS极大值，但尚不具现象学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找自上而下（top-down）构造的、可控的四维de Sitter真空，以解决宇宙加速膨胀的理论基础问题，并检验弦论/ M理论中dS空间的存在性。

Method: 通过M理论在Riemann平坦流形 $T^7/\mathbb{Z}_8$ 上的紧化，构造完全显式的四维de Sitter鞍点解，并分析其真空能量、KK能标与哈勃参数比值及对高圈和高导数修正的可控性。

Result: 获得一个真空能量为 $5.02\\cdot 10^{-10}\\,M_\\text{Pl}^4$、KK/哈勃比约 $10^2$、且在高阶修正下可控的四维dS鞍点；这是首个自上而下的四维dS极大值实例。

Conclusion: 该解虽非现象学可行，但验证了Riemann平坦景观中存在可控dS解的可能性，并为寻找更高质量dS鞍点指明若干新方向。

Abstract: We describe a fully explicit, four-dimensional, top-down de Sitter saddle, constructed via compactification of M-theory on the Riemann-flat manifold $T^7/\mathbb{Z}_8$. The solution has a vacuum energy of $5.02\cdot 10^{-10}\,M_\text{Pl}^4$, a hierarchy $m_{KK}/H\sim 10^{2}$, and is under control with respect to higher-loop and higher-derivative corrections. While the solution is the first example of a top-down dS maximum in four dimensions, it is far from being phenomenologically viable. The solution also suggests several promising directions in the search for higher-quality dS saddles in the Riemann-flat Landscape, which we describe.

</details>


### [110] [Zero- and Finite-Temperature Casimir Effect in a Fracton Gauge Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16263)
*Celio R. Muniz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了自由迹满标量荷fracton规范场在零温和有限温度下两个平行反射边界之间的Casimir相互作用，通过变分分析确定了能约束所有五个物理极化态的边界值问题，并揭示了其多支谱结构对Casimir效应的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高阶规范场（特别是fracton规范场）中多支激发模式对量子真空和热Casimir效应的独特影响，以理解其与传统电磁场行为的差异。

Method: 采用变分法构建一致的边界条件以约束rank-two规范场的五个物理极化；分析零温和有限温下的频谱结构、Matsubara模求和及热自由能与压强的渐近行为。

Result: 零温Casimir压强可达理想电磁值的约2.21倍；有限温下出现双支热权重导致的分裂交叉行为；高低温极限分别体现速度加权量子涨落与五极化经典计数，高温比值恒为2.5。

Conclusion: Casimir效应可作为探测高阶规范场多支谱结构的直接光谱指纹，揭示其从速度加权量子行为到经典模式计数的转变。

Abstract: We investigate the zero- and finite-temperature Casimir interaction of the free trace-full scalar-charge fracton gauge field between two parallel reflecting boundaries. A variational analysis determines a consistent boundary-value problem that confines all five physical polarizations of the rank-two field. The spectrum separates into three faster and two slower propagating modes. At zero temperature, the vacuum interaction weights the modes by their propagation speeds; when the fast-branch speed is set equal to the speed of light, the resulting Casimir pressure is approximately 2.21 times the ideal electromagnetic value. At finite temperature, the two branches acquire different thermal weights and generate a split crossover. The normalized free energy and pressure follow distinct crossover profiles but approach the same classical high-temperature ratio of 2.5 relative to ideal electromagnetism, because the Matsubara zero mode counts the five confined polarizations independently of their propagation speeds. We derive the exact thermal interaction, its low- and high-temperature limits, and the dependence of the crossover scale on the effective gauge-field velocity. The transition from velocity-weighted quantum fluctuations to classical mode counting provides a direct spectral signature of the multi-branch structure of the higher-rank gauge field.

</details>


### [111] [Edge observables in Maxwell theory on null boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16400)
*Dušan Đorđević,Olivera Miskovic,Antonia Montecinos,Tatjana Vukašinac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维Maxwell理论在零边界上的边缘观测量，发现除了通常的U(1)电荷外，还存在一个与零边界内禀对称性相关的第二边缘观测量；二者构成中心扩展代数，在傅里叶模式下退化为两个具有相反水平的独立阿贝尔Kac-Moody代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三维Maxwell理论在不同零边界（如零无穷、黑洞视界、宇宙学视界）上的边缘观测量及其代数结构，以揭示其普适性。

Method: 采用适配于零叶状结构的哈密顿形式，在Minkowski时空、BTZ黑洞和de Sitter空间三种背景上分析零边界上的边缘观测量。

Result: 在所有三种背景下均发现两个良好定义的准局域边缘电荷，它们构成中心扩展代数，并在傅里叶模式下表现为两个具有相反水平的阿贝尔Kac-Moody代数。

Conclusion: 相同的中心扩展边缘结构既出现在渐近零边界（如零无穷），也出现在有限距离零边界（如黑洞或宇宙学视界），表明该结构具有普适性。

Abstract: We study edge observables in three-dimensional Maxwell theory on null boundaries using a Hamiltonian formulation adapted to null foliations. We consider three backgrounds: Minkowski spacetime, the BTZ black hole, and de Sitter space, where the relevant boundaries are, respectively, null infinity, the black hole horizon, and the cosmological horizon. In all three cases we find, besides the usual $U(1)$ charge, a second edge observable associated with an intrinsic symmetry on the null boundary. The two charges are well defined quasilocally. They form a centrally extended algebra which, in Fourier modes, becomes two independent Abelian Kac-Moody algebras with opposite levels. Thus, the same centrally extended edge structure appears at both asymptotic and finite-distance null boundaries.

</details>


### [112] [Stringy Gauge Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16455)
*Gerardo Aldazabal,Eduardo Andrés,Lucía M. Cabrera,Martín Mayo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个能描述异质弦环面紧化中模空间特定点处弦论规范对称性增强-破缺效应的场论作用量，通过加倍周期坐标和非对易积实现背景无关的非阿贝尔结构编码，并提出场变换以恢复增强点处的标准非阿贝尔规范变换，同时将其与广义DFT联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为描述异质弦环面紧化中模空间特定点发生的弦论规范对称性增强与破缺现象，需一个能统一处理质量态（含至多一个振子激发）且背景无关的场论框架。

Method: 基于三阶相互作用与对偶对称性构造场论作用量；引入加倍周期坐标与非对易乘积编码非阿贝尔结构；通过广义模展开显化模空间依赖；提出保留扇区的场变换，并分析其在增强点及一般点下的规范变换性质与标量导数结构协变性；最后将该构造与具扩展切空间的受控DFT关联。

Result: 得到一个截断但自洽的场论模型：在增强点可还原标准非阿贝尔规范变换；标量导数结构具有协变性；能容纳含至多一个振子激发的质量/无质量态表示；并成功与广义DFT建立对应——增强时KK模式对易子复现增强规范代数，非增强时通过Cartan基旋转仍保持相同代数结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为弦紧化中对称性增强/破缺提供了一个有前景的低能有效场论描述雏形，虽为截断版本，但已具备关键特征，并架起了传统场论、非对易几何与广义几何（DFT）之间的桥梁。

Abstract: A field theory action, able to incorporate stringy gauge symmetry enhancement-breaking effects, which occur at particular points of moduli space in a heterotic string toroidal compactification framework, was previously constructed from three-point interactions and duality symmetries. Via doubled periodic coordinates and a non-commutative product, it encodes the non-Abelian structure in a background-independent form. Moduli-space dependence becomes manifest upon performing a generalized mode expansion. Here we further elaborate on this construction. In particular, we propose field transformations for the retained sectors that make up the foundations of a potential symmetry of the action and that, when mode expanded at enhancement points in moduli space, contain the usual non-Abelian gauge transformations. We also examine the covariance of the corresponding scalar derivative structures. Although the proposal must be regarded as a truncated version of a full, presently unknown field-theory description, it already encodes representations of massless and massive states containing up to one oscillator excitation. Finally, we relate the construction to gauged DFT with an extended tangent space by associating generalized Kaluza-Klein modes considered here with additional generalized-frame directions. At enhancement, their brackets reproduce the enhanced gauge algebra; away from enhancement, the same algebraic structure is recovered by rotating the Cartan basis.

</details>


### [113] [Unitarity and the Forward Direction in Theories with Long-Range Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16896)
*Luke Lippstreu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了长程力理论中前向散射振幅积分和幺正性约束的红外模糊性问题，提出了一种结合修正分布结构与扭曲波微扰理论（DWPT）的方法，避免了红外尺度依赖，并在非相对论模型中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在长程力（如库仑力）的理论中，前向散射振幅积分及由此导出的幺正性约束常出现红外模糊性，导致有效场论（EFT）耦合常数的界限依赖于任意红外尺度，缺乏物理意义。

Method: 采用一个可精确求解的非相对论模型作为基准；引入扭曲波微扰理论（DWPT），将库仑动力学严格处理，并修正长程振幅的分布结构，从而获得具有良好分波投影、无虚假红外发散的振幅；将模型视为带紫外截断Λ_EFT的EFT，推导截断依赖的普适界限。

Result: DWPT方法在各阶展开中均不引入红外尺度；截断依赖界限在Λ_EFT→∞极限下快速收敛至精确界限（四阶达0.002%精度）；每一阶DWPT自动重求和无穷多短程微扰图，将多对数函数与椭圆积分等复杂表达式简化为紧凑形式，甚至无需显式积分。

Conclusion: DWPT结合分布结构修正，是处理长程相互作用下幺正性约束和EFT界限问题的可靠且更简洁的框架，有望推广至相对论性及更广义的长程力场景。

Abstract: Integrating scattering amplitudes over the forward direction, and consequently the unitarity constraints one extracts from such integrals, can appear ambiguous in theories with long-range forces. Standard techniques for bounding EFT couplings then typically produce bounds that depend on an arbitrary infrared scale. We study a non-relativistic model in which the standard techniques produce such an ambiguous bound, but which is simple enough that the exact bound can also be derived non-perturbatively and shown to involve no infrared scale. We then show how to derive bounds perturbatively, with no infrared scale entering at any stage. This requires two ingredients: accounting for the modified distributional structure of long-range amplitudes, and using distorted-wave perturbation theory (DWPT), which treats the Coulomb dynamics exactly. Together they give amplitudes with well-defined partial-wave projections and no spurious infrared divergences. Interpreting the model as an EFT with an ultraviolet cutoff $Λ_{\rm EFT}$, we derive cutoff-dependent bounds valid for arbitrary UV completions. Taking $Λ_{\rm EFT}\to\infty$ at each order yields bounds that rapidly converge to the exact bound, reaching $0.002\%$ accuracy at fourth order in the expansion. Finally, we demonstrate that every order of the DWPT expansion resums infinitely many Feynman diagrams of short-range perturbation theory, which individually evaluate to multiple polylogarithms and complete elliptic integrals, into a compact expression. At the orders we compute, no integration is even required, suggesting that DWPT may offer a simpler representation of scattering amplitudes than standard perturbation theory.

</details>


### [114] [Positivity Bounds in $\mathcal N =1$ Supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16924)
*Jingxuan Bu,Jiayin Gu,Runqing Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将EFT的自举框架扩展到N=1超对称理论，利用超对称Ward恒等式导出不同2→2过程Wilson系数间的相互约束，并给出维数为8的算符在标量、费米子和规范场四点相互作用中的正定性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能有效场论需满足局域性、幺正性、解析性和洛伦兹不变性以保证其存在一致的紫外完备；超对称性通过超庞加莱代数关联玻色与费米自由度，亟需将传统正定性约束推广至超对称情形。

Method: 基于2→2前向弹性散射振幅的色散关系，结合N=1超对称Ward恒等式，推导维数8算符对应的不同四点过程Wilson系数之间的线性约束关系，并分析其导致的正定性边界依赖性。

Result: 明确了标量、费米子与规范场间四点相互作用的维数8算符的正定性边界，并揭示这些边界因超对称Ward恒等式而相互关联；指出该代数框架可自然延拓至圈图超振幅与高点过程。

Conclusion: 超对称性显著改变EFT中Wilson系数的正定性结构，SWIs使原本独立的约束变为耦合约束，为超对称EFT提供统一的自举分析视角。

Abstract: For a low-energy Effective Field Theory (EFT) to admit a consistent ultraviolet (UV) completion, it must adhere to the fundamental principles of locality, unitarity, analyticity, and Lorentz invariance. This leads to positivity constraints on certain Wilson coefficients via dispersion relations of $2\rightarrow2$ forward elastic amplitudes, which carry significant implications for both theoretical consistency and experimental phenomenology. In this work, we extend this bootstrap framework to $\mathcal N = 1$ supersymmetric theories, where the super-Poincaré algebra relates the bosonic and fermionic degrees of freedom within a single supermultiplet. Supersymmetric Ward identities (SWIs) enforce exact linear relations among component scattering amplitudes. Consequently, the Wilson coefficients associated with different $2\rightarrow2$ processes are mutually constrained, rendering their respective positivity bounds dependent. Focusing on dimension-8 operators, we explicitly show the positivity bounds on quartic interactions involving scalars, fermions and gauge fields, and demonstrate how they are related as a direct consequence of the SWIs. Furthermore, we expect that the algebraic nature of this framework naturally extends to loop-level superamplitudes and higher-point processes, providing a unified bootstrap perspective on supersymmetric EFTs.

</details>


### [115] [Constraints on Kasner Exponents from Holography and Energy Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16980)
*Zi-Hao Li,Run-Qiu Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了近奇点Kasner标度是否可作为全息对偶中内部几何的诊断工具，通过分析CV、CA、HM熵和热a函数等内部敏感观测量，推导出Kasner指数的代数约束，并指出经典能量条件（如零能量条件和主能量条件）可确保这些指数落在全息允许区域内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自下而上的全息理论中，判断一个给定的体有效理论是否存在一致的全息对偶是一个核心问题；本文旨在探索黑洞内部近奇点的Kasner标度能否作为基于内部敏感全息探针的有效诊断工具。

Method: 通过分析控制内部敏感可观测量（包括复杂度=体积CV、复杂度=作用量CA的体部贡献、Hartman-Maldacena熵、热a函数）的度规组合，推导出Kasner指数(p_t,p_s)在半经典相关区域（如有限半径晚期CV与HM分支、有限CA复杂度、有限近奇点热a函数）中的代数约束界限。

Result: 得到了Kasner指数需满足的不等式，划定了Kasner空间中仅凭终端标度即可实现上述行为的区域；并证明经典能量条件（零能条件和主能量条件）是保证Kasner指数落入全息允许区域的简单充分判据。

Conclusion: 建立了经典体能量条件与全息探针所选取的内部几何之间的直接联系，表明Kasner标度可作为自下而上全息理论中一种互补的诊断工具。

Abstract: A central question in bottom-up holography is whether a given bulk effective theory admits a consistent holographic dual. In this work, we explore whether the near-singularity Kasner scaling of planar AdS black hole interiors can serve as a useful diagnostic based on interior-sensitive holographic probes. By examining the metric combinations that control interior-sensitive observables, including Complexity=Volume (CV), the bulk contribution to Complexity=Action (CA), Hartman-Maldacena (HM) entropy, and the thermal $a$-function, we derive algebraic bounds on the Kasner exponents $(p_t,p_s)$ for the relevant semi-classical regimes: finite-radius late-time branches of CV and HM, finite CA complexity, and a finite near-singularity thermal $a$-function. The resulting inequalities delineate the corresponding regions of Kasner space in which the terminal scaling alone is sufficient to realize these behaviors. Furthermore, we demonstrate that classical energy conditions, specifically the null and dominant energy conditions, provide simple sufficient criteria ensuring that the Kasner exponents fall within these holographically allowed regions. These results establish a direct connection between classical bulk energy conditions and the interior geometry selected by holographic probes, and suggest that Kasner scaling can provide a complementary diagnostic in bottom-up holography.

</details>


### [116] [Carrollian Conformal Dynamics and Flat-Space Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17086)
*Simon Pekar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Carroll几何的共形动力学度规-仿射表述，通过超动量追踪连接自由度，并在渐近平直时空的零无穷处导出了引力质量与角动量演化的标准方程，为平直空间全息提供了边界本征的几何实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近平直时空的共形边界是类光的，具有Carroll结构而非Lorentz结构，这从根本上改变了对称性、变分原理与演化方程之间的关系；传统Lorentz Levi-Civita联络无法容纳该边界上独立的联络自由度（如Bondi剪切），需新框架描述。

Method: 采用度规-仿射（metric-affine）方法构建共形动力学，引入超动量以编码无挠Carroll联络中独立的横向对称部分；结合局部Carroll提升、旋转、Weyl变换与微分同胚不变性，导出协变演化方程；在Penrose共形紧化框架下，将Carroll动量与超动量对应于Bondi数据，并固定BMS参考系。

Result: 成功复现了质量与角动量方面（mass and angular-momentum aspects）的标准演化方程；给出了引力演化方程的纯边界本征推导；实现了平直空间全息中辐射自由度的几何刻画。

Conclusion: Carroll几何是描述渐近平直时空共形边界物理的自然语言；度规-仿射框架及其超动量变量为理解零无穷处的动力学与对称性提供了统一、内在且几何清晰的基础。

Abstract: The conformal boundary of asymptotically flat spacetime is null and therefore carries a Carrollian rather than Lorentzian geometry. This changes the relation between symmetry, variational principles and evolution equations in an essential way: unlike a Lorentzian Levi-Civita connection, a torsion-free Carrollian connection compatible with the conformal structure contains genuine independent data. We develop a metric-affine formulation of conformal dynamics that keeps track of these connection degrees of freedom through hypermomenta. Local Carroll boosts, rotations, Weyl transformations and diffeomorphisms then imply a set of covariant evolution equations. We apply this framework to Einstein gravity in four-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetimes. Penrose's conformal compactification identifies null infinity as a conformal Carrollian manifold; the undetermined transverse symmetric part of its compatible connection is the asymptotic Bondi shear. After fixing a Bondi-van der Burg-Metzner-Sachs (BMS) frame and identifying the Carrollian momenta and hypermomenta with the Bondi data, the general Carrollian evolution equations reproduce the standard evolution equations for the mass and angular-momentum aspects. The result provides a boundary-intrinsic derivation of the gravitational evolution equations and a geometric realization of radiative degrees of freedom in flat-space holography. This is a proceedings contribution to the "Athens Workshop in Theoretical Physics: 10th Anniversary", held at the National and Kapodistrian University of Athens on December 17-19 2025.

</details>


### [117] [Supersymmetric extension of the Hull--Lambert--Sen chiral boson on the 2-Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17161)
*Amine El Amri,Pietro Antonio Grassi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hull-Lambert-Sen手征玻色子模型的超对称扩展，利用Hull发展的双度规框架，在二维环面上显式构造了该理论，并分析了其与外部源的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为拓展手征玻色子模型至超对称情形，并利用双度规框架实现其可处理的量子化。

Method: 基于Hull的双度规形式，构造超对称化的手征玻色子理论，并在二维环面上进行具体实现与外源耦合分析。

Result: 成功构建了手征玻色子模型的超对称扩展，并验证其在双度规框架下具有良好性质及外源耦合行为。

Conclusion: Hull的双度规框架天然兼容超对称推广，为手征玻色子的量子化提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We present a supersymmetric extension of the Hull-Lambert-Sen chiral boson model. The two-metric formulation developed by Hull provides a powerful and tractable framework for the quantization of chiral bosons, and we demonstrate that it admits a natural supersymmetric generalization. We construct the theory explicitly on the two-torus and analyze its coupling to external sources.

</details>


### [118] [Generalized complexity and dynamical response in holographic Vaidya spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17182)
*Mojtaba Shahbazi,Monireh Emami*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了平滑Vaidya几何中的'复杂度=任意量'猜想，使用Weyl平方泛函作为主要候选，并通过数值分析和Fefferman-Graham展开揭示了动态解与静态解的复杂度差异，最终建立了广义全息复杂度与边界应力张量动力学响应之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究平滑Vaidya几何中'复杂度=任意量'猜想的有效性，特别是动态时空下复杂度的演化行为及其与边界物理的关联。

Method: 采用Weyl平方泛函作为复杂度候选；对4维和5维Reissner-Nordström及5维Gauss-Bonnet Vaidya几何进行数值分析；利用Fefferman-Graham展开比较静态与动态解的复杂度差异；结合线性响应理论和Kramers-Kronig关系分析应力张量响应。

Result: 发现Vaidya解中复杂度在r和v方向均演化（'双重复杂度'）；FG展开中前四项系数在静态与动态解间相消，首非零贡献出现在第五项，由边界应力张量及其导数控制；Vaidya淬火期间导数项编码边界态的时间依赖响应；该响应在线性响应下由推迟应力张量关联子及谱密度表征。

Conclusion: 广义全息复杂度与边界应力张量的动力学响应存在深刻联系，在线性响应 regime 下可由应力张量谱密度完全刻画。

Abstract: We investigate "Complexity=Anything" for smooth Vaidya geometries, using the Weyl squared functional as the most common candidate for our study. Our numerical analysis of candidates in 4- and 5-dimensional Reissner-Nordström (RN) and 5-dimensional Gauss-Bonnet (GB) shows evolution in both $r$ and $v$ ("doubled complexity"), unlike static solutions that depend only on $r$. We then study the difference between the complexity of static and dynamical solutions in the asymptotically AdS regime using a Fefferman-Graham expansion. By expanding the bulk metric, extremal embedding, induced metric, normal vector, and extrinsic curvature simultaneously, we show that the first four FG coefficients cancel between the two geometries. In contrast, the first nonvanishing contribution occurs at the fifth coefficient and is controlled by the boundary stress tensor and its derivatives. During the Vaidya quench, the derivative contribution encodes the time-dependent response of the boundary state. In linear response, this response is governed by the retarded stress-tensor correlator and, through generalized Kramers-Kronig relations, can be represented in terms of its spectral density. We thus identify a connection between generalized holographic complexity and the dynamical stress-tensor response, which in the linear-response regime can be represented in terms of the corresponding stress-tensor spectral density.

</details>


### [119] [Cohomological Aspects of Entanglement Entropy: From Information Theory to Noncommutative Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17195)
*Radoslav C. Rashkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于上同调的纠缠熵理论框架，统一了信息论、算子代数与非交换几何视角，并通过Connes循环双复形和Tomita-Takesaki模理论，为量子场论与全息原理中的纠缠提供了严格数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一信息论、算子代数与非交换几何对纠缠熵的理解，并为量子场论（尤其是Type III因子情形）中缺乏密度矩阵时的纠缠提供严格数学描述。

Method: 以信息熵作为1-上循环为起点，推广至量子情形下的Hochschild与循环上同调；利用条件期望将纠缠复形嵌入Connes循环双复形；借助Tomita-Takesaki模理论及Connes-Radon-Nikodym上循环刻画相对纠缠。

Result: 建立了纠缠上同调作为von Neumann代数到其子代数限制映射的核；确认Connes-Radon-Nikodym上循环为编码相对纠缠的基本对象；该框架自然适用于无局部密度矩阵的Type III代数。

Conclusion: 该上同调框架为量子多体系统、量子场论与全息对偶中的纠缠提供了普适、内蕴且严格的数学语言。

Abstract: We develop a cohomological framework for entanglement entropy that unifies perspectives from information theory, operator algebras, and noncommutative geometry. Starting from the information-theoretic characterization of entropy as a 1-cocycle, we show how this structure generalizes to the quantum setting through Hochschild and cyclic cohomology. A central result is the embedding of an entanglement complex into the Connes cyclic bicomplex via a conditional expectation, identifying entanglement cohomology as the kernel of the restriction map from a von Neumann algebra to its subalgebra. The Tomita-Takesaki modular theory provides the dynamical structure, with the Connes-Radon-Nikodym cocycle serving as the fundamental object encoding relative entanglement. This framework naturally accommodates Type III von Neumann algebras, where no local density matrix exists, offering a rigorous foundation for entanglement in quantum field theory and holography.

</details>


### [120] [Low-energy brane decoupling in AdS flux vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17342)
*Fien Apers,Noelia Sánchez González*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过标量波吸收概率研究了产生AdS通量真空的膜与渐近体空间的解耦行为，发现对于任意大于二维的AdS真空及任意势陡度，低能极限下吸收概率趋于零，表明膜与体空间解耦；并在DGKT、AdS₃及KKLT等典型模型中进行了验证和阐释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS通量真空中源膜与体空间的解耦机制，对AdS/CFT对应及弦论真空选择具有重要意义。

Method: 基于单模场截断，利用标量波吸收概率分析膜-体耦合强度，并结合具体AdS真空模型（如DGKT、AdS₃、KKLT）进行计算与类比。

Result: 证明在任意大于2维且任意势陡度下，低能极限吸收概率为零，即膜完全解耦；对DGKT模型得到吸收概率标度为ω^{27/7}，有效横向维数为20/7，并发现其模场势可类比于(4+13/7)维引力紧化在13/7维球上的结果。

Conclusion: AdS通量真空中的源膜在低能下普遍与体空间解耦，该结论具有普适性，并可通过有效高维引力图像加以理解。

Abstract: We study the decoupling of branes sourcing AdS flux vacua from the asymptotic bulk using scalar-wave absorption probabilities. In a one-modulus truncation, the result depends only on the dimension of the AdS vacuum and on the asymptotic steepness of the scalar potential. We find that for any steepness and for any dimension greater than two the absorption probability vanishes in the low-energy limit, showing that the branes decouple from the bulk. We illustrate the general analysis in several scale-separated AdS vacua. For DGKT we find $P_{\rm abs}\simω^{27/7}$ and an effective transverse dimension $d_{\rm eff}=20/7$. Interestingly, the scalar potential along the brane-induced moduli trajectory agrees with that obtained by compactifying a hypothetical $(4+13/7)$-dimensional gravity theory on a $13/7$-dimensional sphere threaded by flux. We also apply the analysis to scale-separated $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ examples and to the simplest one-modulus KKLT model.

</details>


### [121] [Topological Modular Forms Constrain Threshold States in Extremal $\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_3$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17359)
*Yutaka Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个对极值N=1 AdS3谱的拓扑约束，该约束不能由三个偶自旋结构配分函数的一阶模协方差推出；在Ramond Witten指数满足拓扑模形式可除性假设下，证明了在中心荷c=12n（n为无穷多个奇数）时，极值全纯N=1超共形场论必须在BTZ阈值处具有奇数个Neveu-Schwarz原初算符，从而排除了无阈值原初算符的严格假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索极值N=1 AdS3谱中未被一阶模协方差所涵盖的额外拓扑约束。

Method: 基于拓扑模形式对Ramond Witten指数的可除性假设，分析中心荷c=12n（n为奇数）下的极值全纯N=1超共形场论谱结构。

Result: 证明对于无穷多个奇数n，在c=12n时，极值全纯N=1超共形场论必须在BTZ阈值处具有奇数个Neveu-Schwarz原初算符。

Conclusion: 无阈值原初算符的严格假设被证伪，揭示了新的拓扑约束。

Abstract: We identify a topological constraint on extremal $\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_3$ spectra that is not implied by genus-one modular covariance of the three even spin structure partition functions. Assuming the expected topological modular forms divisibility of the Ramond Witten index, we show that for infinitely many odd $n$ at central charge $c=12n$, an extremal holomorphic $\mathcal{N}=1$ superconformal field theory requires an odd number of Neveu--Schwarz primaries at the BTZ threshold. Thus the strict ansatz with no threshold primaries is excluded for this infinite family.

</details>


### [122] [Channel-Resolved High-Overtone Quasinormal Modes Decode Kasner Scaling inside Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17426)
*Zi-Qing Xiao,Jun Nian,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过黑洞高阶准正规模谱独立重构其内部Kasner度规指数的新方法，无需预先假设Kasner约束或依赖全息原理，并验证了该方法在多种黑洞解（如5维Schwarzschild、Schwarzschild–AdS₅及Gibbons–Maeda族）中的有效性；谱中分数幂修正项直接反映了视界后近奇点区域的局部几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内部奇点附近的几何结构（特别是Kasner标度行为）是广义相对论和量子引力的关键问题，但传统方法往往依赖于全息对偶或强先验假设，缺乏直接、普适的探测手段。

Method: 利用高阶准正规模（high-overtone quasinormal modes）的渐近谱结构，分离出全局谱数据与由近奇点Kasner几何决定的局部分数幂代数修正；通过分析谱中不同结构分别提取时间与空间Kasner指标，并进行独立重构。

Result: 成功在5维渐近平直Schwarzschild、平面Schwarzschild–AdS₅及连续变化的Gibbons–Maeda黑洞族中实现了Kasner指数的独立谱重构；重构结果自动满足Kasner关系，构成非平凡的几何一致性检验；证实分数幂谱修正直接编码近奇点局部几何。

Conclusion: 高阶准正规模谱不仅包含黑洞外部信息，更蕴含可被精确解码的内部奇点附近几何特征；该方法为不依赖全息或先验对称性假设地探测黑洞内部提供了首个通用谱学途径。

Abstract: The high-overtone quasinormal-mode spectrum encodes the near-singularity Kasner scaling of black-hole interiors. We show that distinct structures in this spectrum independently determine the temporal and spatial Kasner grades, allowing the corresponding metric exponents to be reconstructed without imposing Kasner constraints or invoking holography. We demonstrate this independent reconstruction at the fixed Kasner point of five-dimensional asymptotically flat Schwarzschild and planar Schwarzschild--AdS$_5$, and then extend it to a continuously varying Gibbons--Maeda black-hole family. The reconstructed exponents satisfy the Kasner relation a posteriori, providing a nontrivial geometric consistency check. More generally, the high-overtone spectrum separates into global spectral data and local algebraic corrections whose fractional powers are fixed by the near-singularity Kasner geometry. These fractional corrections therefore provide a direct spectral probe of the local geometry behind the horizon.

</details>


### [123] [On the relaxation dynamics of non-equilibrium quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17447)
*Matthias Carosi,Björn Garbrecht,Silvia Pla,Nils Wagner,Edward Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了近局域平衡下相互作用量子系统中近似守恒荷的弛豫行为，基于Zubarev非平衡统计算符方法和局域平衡构造，导出了弛豫率由平衡关联函数决定的普适规律，并将其与扩散行为联系起来，最后通过电弱B+L抹除和标量模型验证了该理论与线性化玻尔兹曼方程的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解近似守恒荷在相互作用量子系统中的弛豫机制，特别是在近局域平衡条件下的动力学行为，弥合非平衡统计物理与动理学描述之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 采用Zubarev非平衡统计算符方法（简化为单个非守恒荷情形），结合局域平衡构造，在弱荷破坏、多尺度分离和微观记忆丢失等物理假设下推导弛豫律；进一步推广至空间依赖情形，得到扩散-弛豫方程，并以电弱B+L过程和微扰标量模型进行验证。

Result: 得出近似守恒荷的弛豫率由电荷破坏算符的平衡关联函数决定；建立了弛豫率与对应荷扩散系数的定量关系；导出含空间变化的扩散-弛豫方程；在具体模型中验证了该结果与线性化玻尔兹曼方程完全一致。

Conclusion: Zubarev方法与局域平衡构造在物理本质和结果上等价，揭示了弛豫现象的普适统计基础；该框架统一了基于平衡关联函数的场论描述与基于碰撞项的动理学描述。

Abstract: We investigate the relaxation of an approximately conserved charge in interacting quantum systems close to local equilibrium. To this end, we provide a pedagogical review of Zubarev's non-equilibrium statistical operator approach in the minimal setting of a single non-conserved charge and apply it to the problem at hand. We explicitly highlight the physical assumptions that lead to a local relaxation law: weak charge violation, a separation between the short timescale of microscopic correlations and the much longer timescale of charge relaxation, and the resulting loss of microscopic memory. Under these conditions, the leading relaxation rate is determined by an equilibrium correlation function of the charge-violating operator. We show that the same result follows from a simpler local-equilibrium construction based on the system's evolution over an intermediate timescale, providing a direct alternative for practical calculations and making the common physical ingredients of the two approaches explicit. Beyond the decay law itself, we relate the relaxation rate to the equilibrium diffusion of the same charge. We then allow the charge density to vary in space, which leads to a diffusion-relaxation equation. Finally, we illustrate the formalism through electroweak $\mathrm{B+L}$ washout and a perturbative scalar model, where agreement with the linearized Boltzmann equation establishes a direct connection between equilibrium-correlator and kinetic descriptions.

</details>


### [124] [Exact Black Branes in General Dimensions in Higher-Curvature Scalar-Tensor Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17528)
*Tianhao Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在无势稀释子耦合的Lovelock-Horndeski理论中构造了两类中性平面黑洞膜解，揭示了标量场动力学与曲率之间的几何补偿机制，并分析了其维度延拓与共形性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受异质紧化启发，高维曲率与标量动力学相关联，促使构建含标量场的广义引力理论以研究黑洞热力学与几何结构。

Method: 基于无势稀释子耦合的Lovelock-Horndeski理论，通过精确求解场方程获得两类中性平面黑膜解；结合Wald熵公式、标量因子在视界处的行为及耦合系数分析，研究其热力学稳定性与几何特性。

Result: 发现两类解：线性族（共形平坦，AdS尺度自由）和分数族（Weyl弯曲，固定AdS尺度）；视界半径与温度可变，但Wald熵恒正不变，源于视界处指数标量因子对面积增长的精确补偿；两族均可由四维延拓至任意高维，且不能由实单标量平环面约化实现。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了标量-几何补偿机制在广义高阶引力中的普适性，为理解黑洞熵的微观起源及紧化机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: We obtain two families of neutral planar black branes in a potential-free dilaton-coupled Lovelock--Horndeski theory. Heterotic compactification relates higher-dimensional curvature to scalar dynamics and motivates the interaction structure. At fixed scalar zero mode, the horizon radius and temperature vary while the complete stationary Wald entropy remains constant and positive. The exponential scalar factor at the horizon exactly compensates the area growth, giving this behavior a direct geometric explanation. An exact coefficient analysis determines the two four-dimensional branches and their continuation to every higher dimension through closed coupling relations. The linear family is conformally flat and leaves the AdS scale free at fixed couplings; the fractional family is Weyl curved and fixes that scale. The common kinetic relation also excludes their realization through a real one-scalar flat-torus reduction of a metric--dilaton parent.

</details>

### [125] [$Λ$CDM in a courtroom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16097)
*Ofer Lahav,Paul Shah*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文综述了宇宙学常数Λ（暗能量）在当前宇宙学标准模型中的地位，评估了其为常数还是动力学暗能量（DDE）的观测证据，并展望未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来对星系团聚、Ia型超新星和宇宙微波背景的测量对ΛCDM模型中Λ为常数的假设提出了挑战，亟需系统评估常数Λ与动力学暗能量（DDE）的证据强弱。

Method: 采用综述与批判性分析方法，整合近期多信使（星系巡天、超新星、CMB）观测结果，对比常数Λ与DDE模型的拟合优度与理论自洽性。

Result: 目前观测数据仍与常数Λ高度一致，但部分异常（如哈勃张力、S8张力）为DDE提供了有限但日益增长的支持；尚未有决定性证据推翻Λ为常数的主流观点。

Conclusion: Λ作为主导宇宙演化的成分仍最简且最符合现有数据，但DDE不能被排除；未来十年的大型巡天项目（如Euclid、LSST、CMB-S4）将显著提升区分能力，可能最终判定暗能量的本质。

Abstract: The cosmological constant $Λ$, and its generalization as Dark Energy, makes up roughly 70% of the present-epoch universe and has been part of the dominant $Λ$CDM cosmological paradigm for more than a quarter of a century. It has been put on trial by recent measurements of galaxy clustering, Type Ia supernovae, and the cosmic microwave background. In this Perspective we evaluate the strength of the evidence for a constant $Λ$, versus Dynamical Dark Energy (DDE) in the light of recent developments. We also assess what the future holds both for our Universe and our understanding of its most mysterious component.

</details>


### [126] [A Dark-matter Origin of Little Red Dots: Early Seeding and Super-Bondi Accretion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16122)
*Hua-Peng Gu,Fangzhou Jiang,Xian Chen,Ran Li,Zi-Xiang Jia*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出，自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）可通过引力热坍缩形成恒星级黑洞种子，并通过持续的超邦迪吸积使其快速成长为超大质量黑洞，从而自然解释早期宇宙中‘小红点’（LRDs）的异常特性，无需依赖重子物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释早期宇宙中‘小红点’（LRDs）——即具有极高的黑洞-恒星质量比、高空间密度和过早出现的吸积超大质量黑洞——对传统重子依赖黑洞种子模型构成的严重挑战。

Method: 采用完全相对论性、非平衡态的晕演化模型，追踪自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）晕从初始坍缩形成黑洞种子到后续暗物质吸积的完整引力热演化过程。

Result: 在再电离前形成的高浓度SIDM晕中，引力热坍缩可在数亿年内高效产生恒星级黑洞种子；SIDM中的热传导可维持长期超邦迪吸积流，使种子在LRD时期成长为超大质量黑洞；该机制预测的SMBH数量与红移分布与观测到的LRDs一致。

Conclusion: 超大质量黑洞可在显著星系形成之前主要由暗物质播种并组装，这不仅为LRDs提供了有力物理解释，也为探测暗物质微观物理性质提供了新观测途径。

Abstract: The "Little red dots" (LRDs) are a population of accreting supermassive black holes (SMBHs) in the early Universe which often exhibit undermassive or even undetectable stellar hosts. Their early emergence, high space density, and extremely large black-hole-to-stellar mass ratios pose a serious challenge to conventional seeding scenarios that rely on baryon for both the formation and growth of black holes. Here we demonstrate that the above anomalies can be naturally resolved if dark matter is self-interacting. We apply a fully relativistic, non-equilibrium halo-evolution model, first developed in our earlier work, to trace the complete gravothermal evolution of self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) halos, from the initial collapse into BH seeds to the subsequent accretion of dark matter. We find that in highly concentrated halos assembled before reionization, gravothermal collapse efficiently produces stellar-mass black-hole seeds within a few hundred million years. Remarkably, and contrary to standard expectations for dark-matter accretion, heat conduction in SIDM then sustains a prolonged super-Bondi inflow that drives these seeds to supermassive scale by the LRD epoch, without baryonic assistance. The halo conditions required for completing these processes, together with the probability of avoiding major mergers that disrupt gravothermal evolution, result in an SMBH population consistent with the observed abundance and redshift distribution of LRDs. Our findings establish a pathway in which SMBHs are seeded and assembled primarily from dark matter, well before substantial galaxies form around them, thereby offering both a compelling physical explanation for LRDs and a new observational probe of dark-matter microphysics.

</details>


### [127] [CMB-HD Foregrounds: Simulations, Source Detection, and Foreground Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16128)
*Amanda MacInnis,Joshua Ange,Neelima Sehgal,Joshua A. Kable,Isabelle Blackstad*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率微波天空模拟，展示了CMB-HD巡天中星系外前景（如CIB、射电星系、SZ效应）的去除方法，并评估其对宇宙学参数限制的影响，结果表明前景去除后对ΛCDM等扩展模型参数不确定性影响小于7%，且仍能高精度测量有效中微子种类数N_eff。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持CMB-HD（Cosmic Microwave Background-High Definition）这一超深、超高分辨率微波巡天项目，需准确建模并去除强干扰的星系外前景信号，以保障宇宙学参数（尤其是轻热遗迹粒子如中微子）的精确提取。

Method: 基于现有全天低分辨率模拟生成2.5角秒分辨率、100平方度的微波天空模拟；采用专用算法检测并去除宇宙红外背景（CIB）、射电星系及热SZ效应引起的星系团信号；利用90和148 GHz温度图联合去噪，并结合偏振、T-E交叉谱及偏振重构透镜谱进行残差评估。

Result: 实现95%完备性的源表：CIB（0.008 mJy @ 90 GHz）、射电星系（0.04 mJy @ 148 GHz）；SZ星系团样本在质量阈值M_500c > 5×10¹³ M⊙下近乎完备；去 foreground 后残差功率谱比理想估计高50%，但11参数扩展ΛCDM模型的参数不确定性增幅<7%；σ(N_eff) = 0.0154，可90%置信排除ΔN_eff ≥ 0.027的新粒子。

Conclusion: 所提前景去除方法在真实模拟中表现稳健，对关键宇宙学参数（尤其是N_eff）影响极小；公开代码与模拟数据将助力未来超深高分辨CMB巡天的分析管线开发。

Abstract: We present simulations of the microwave sky at 2.5 arcsecond resolution over 100 square degrees, generated from existing full-sky, lower-resolution simulations, and use them to demonstrate extragalactic foreground removal for a CMB-HD survey. Our cleaning method detects and removes the cosmic infrared background and radio galaxies, yielding source catalogs that are 95% complete down to flux limits of 0.008 and 0.04 mJy at 90 and 148 GHz, respectively. It also identifies and removes galaxy clusters via the thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich effect, producing a cluster sample that is nearly complete above $M_{500c} = 5 \times 10^{13} M_\odot$. After cleaning, the residual foreground-plus-noise power spectrum of the coadded 90 and 148 GHz temperature map is 50% higher than previous idealized estimates, increasing cosmological parameter uncertainties for an 11-parameter $Λ$CDM + $N_\mathrm{eff}$ + $\sum m_ν$ + $T_\mathrm{AGN}$ + $A_\mathrm{kSZ}$ + $n_\mathrm{kSZ}$ model by less than 7%. The small impact on cosmological parameters reflects the strong constraining power of CMB polarization, the temperature-polarization cross spectra, and CMB lensing spectra reconstructed from polarization-only estimators, all of which are minimally affected by extragalactic foregrounds. In particular, the survey remains a sensitive probe of light thermal relic particles, achieving $σ(N_\mathrm{eff}) = 0.0154$, which can exclude any new species ($ΔN_\mathrm{eff} \ge 0.027$) with at least 90% confidence. Our simulation and foreground-removal codes are publicly available and should aid the development of analysis pipelines for ultradeep, ultrahigh-resolution microwave surveys.

</details>


### [128] [Peering Beyond the Veil of Last Scattering: A View of the Universe with CMB Spectral Distortions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16194)
*Bryce Cyr,Nabila Aghanim,Ethan Baker,Elia Stefano Battistelli,Richard Battye,José Luis Bernal,Jens Chluba,Xavier Coulon,William Coulton,Paolo de Bernardis,Eleonora Di Valentino,Guillem Domènech,Sara Evangelista,Giulio Fabbian,Fabio Finelli,J. Colin Hill,Rishi Khatri,Alan Kogut,Guilaine Lagache,Hongwan Liu,Bruno Maffei,Fereshteh Majidi,Abhishek S. Maniyar,C. J. A. P. Martins,Silvia Masi,Jeff McMahon,Daniela Paoletti,Vivian Poulin,Wenzer Qin,Mathieu Remazeilles,José Alberto Rubiño-Martín,Alina Sabyr,Mayuri Sathyanarayana Rao,Carlos Sierra,Jack Singal,Tracy R. Slatyer,Elsa M. Teixeira,Leander Thiele,Léo Vacher,Duncan Watts*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文综述了宇宙微波背景（CMB）谱畸变的物理机制、科学价值及未来实验前景，强调其作为探测宇宙热历史（尤其z ≲ 2×10⁶）的独特高灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CMB频谱是迄今最精确的天然黑体谱，但标准ΛCDM模型预言了尚未被当前观测（如COBE）探测到的微弱谱畸变；这些畸变蕴含再复合前后热历史的关键信息，可延伸探测至最后散射面之外的原初宇宙。

Method: 采用理论综述方法，系统梳理全时期CMB谱畸变的产生机制（包括标准ΛCDM内及新物理机制），并分析各向异性谱畸变所携带的空间信息；同时评估TMS、COSMO、BISOU及空间实验FOSSIL等未来观测项目的探测能力与预期成果。

Result: 预测TMS/COSMO/BISOU将在十年内首次发现单极y型畸变；FOSSIL空间实验有望将COBE/FIRAS灵敏度提升约三个数量级，实现对ΛCDM预言的μ型畸变（源于再复合前等离子体中小尺度声波耗散）的首次探测。

Conclusion: CMB谱畸变是探测高红移（z ≲ 2×10⁶）宇宙热历史的极其灵敏且不可替代的探针，即将迎来突破性观测时代。

Abstract: The frequency spectrum of the cosmic microwave background is the most precise blackbody ever measured in nature, with deviations constrained at the level of almost one part per million from the COBE satellite. Nevertheless, departures away from a perfect blackbody are present in standard $Λ$CDM cosmology, lurking just beneath the surface of our current observational bounds. These spectral distortions provide invaluable information on our thermal history in both the post- and pre-recombination epochs, allowing us to peer beyond last scattering and into the primordial Universe. Here, we present an overview of the underlying physics responsible for generating CMB spectral distortions at all epochs. As an illustration of this rich physics, we review a comprehensive set of mechanisms capable of generating distortions both within and beyond the standard $Λ$CDM paradigm. We also discuss the information that can be gleaned by going beyond the monopole (sky-averaged) spectrum and exploiting the spatial information present in anisotropic spectral distortions. To supplement our discussion of the diverse science of spectral distortions, we provide an overview of the upcoming and proposed experimental landscape. We highlight that the combination of the TMS, COSMO, and BISOU experiments will provide the first discovery of a monopole $y$-type distortion within the coming decade. From space, the proposed FOSSIL experiment is forecasted to improve upon the original COBE/FIRAS measurement by roughly three orders of magnitude in sensitivity, bringing with it the detection of the $Λ$CDM $μ$-type distortion sourced by the dissipation of small scale acoustic modes in the pre-recombination plasma. With transformational measurements on the horizon, CMB spectral distortions offer a uniquely sensitive probe of the thermal history of the Universe at redshifts $z \lesssim 2 \times 10^6$.

</details>


### [129] [Probing multi-state dark matter via optical absorption lines in DESI spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16547)
*Anoma Ganguly,Shadab Alam*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过光学吸收线探测多态暗物质的新方法，利用DESI等光谱巡天数据对前景暗物质晕中的磁偶极跃迁进行搜寻，在3–5 eV能区给出目前最强的截面限制，并指出该方法可补充宇宙学与直接探测的参数空间空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗物质探测手段（如宇宙学观测BBN+CMB和地下直接探测）在特定参数区间（如few MeV质量、O(eV)跃迁能）存在灵敏度盲区；而多态暗物质模型中电磁高阶矩（如磁偶极跃迁）可在背景光源光谱上产生特征吸收线，提供新探针。

Method: 基于两态暗物质模型，假设其通过磁偶极矩与光子耦合，在光学波段（ΔE ∼ O(eV)）产生吸收线；采用DESI早期数据释放的145,087条光谱，匹配GAMA星系群作为前景暗物质晕，进行光谱叠加分析以提取平均光学深度。

Result: 叠加结果光学深度与零一致；在ΔE ∼ 3–5 eV、暗物质质量为few MeV区域，给出2σ截面上限，是当前该参数空间最严格限制；该区域无法被BBN/CMB和直接探测覆盖。

Conclusion: 光谱叠加法是探测电磁相互作用多态暗物质的有效且互补的新途径；DESI全量数据及下一代光谱巡天有望将限制提升一个数量级以上。

Abstract: We search for dark matter through absorption lines imprinted on the spectra of background sources whose sightlines pass through foreground dark matter halos. Such a line is a generic feature of multi-state dark matter models that couple to photons through higher-order electromagnetic moments. We focus on the minimal case of a two-state system with a magnetic dipole transition, in which the ground state absorbs a photon and makes a transition to the excited state. For transition energy $ΔE \sim O \,(\text{eV})$, the line falls in the optical band, within reach of spectroscopic surveys such as DESI. Stacking 145,087 spectra of DESI Early Data Release objects paired with foreground GAMA galaxy groups, we find a stacked optical depth consistent with zero and place $2σ$ upper limits on the magnetic dipole transition cross-section. The limits are strongest at $ΔE \sim$ 3-5 eV for a few MeV dark matter mass in the parameter space that is inaccessible to both cosmological (BBN+CMB) and direct detection experiments. We forecast that the full DESI and the next-generation spectroscopic surveys will tighten these limits by more than an order of magnitude, establishing spectroscopic stacking as a competitive probe of electromagnetically interacting multi-state dark matter.

</details>


### [130] [Cosmological information from field-level analysis in redshift space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16719)
*Julia Stadler*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过基于大尺度结构有效场论的前向模型，对星系团聚进行场级分析，相较于传统的功率谱和双谱组合，在ΛCDM及扩展宇宙学模型中显著提升了宇宙学参数（如原初涨落振幅）的测量精度，但其实现需在观测建模与数值推断技术上取得进一步发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化场级分析相较于当前功率谱与双谱联合分析在扰动理论区域内的精度增益，并评估其在不同宇宙学模型和星系样本下的表现。

Method: 使用LEFTfield前向模型生成类DESI LRG红移空间模拟数据，并基于该模型开展场级分析与功率谱/双谱的Fisher预报对比。

Result: 在ΛCDM模型中场级分析使原初涨落振幅测量精度提升约2倍；在扩展宇宙学模型中，原初振幅精度提升达3倍，ΛCDM偏离参数精度提升达2倍。

Conclusion: 场级分析具有显著宇宙学潜力，但需在观测建模、系统误差处理及数值推断方法上取得实质性进展才能充分实现。

Abstract: The field-level analysis of galaxy clustering with a forward model based on the Effective Field Theory of Large Scale structure provides cosmological measurements at optimal statistical precision which are simultaneously robust with respect to theoretical uncertainties regarding galaxy formation. Yet, the quantitative gains of field-level analysis in the perturbative regime over the current state-of-the-art, set by the combination of power- and bispectrum, are not obvious, and they depend on analysis choices, the cosmological model, and the observed galaxy population. We therefore quantify the precision improvement from field-level analysis over power- and bispectrum from redshift-space snapshots that closely resemble the DESI LRG sample. Our mock data are created with the LEFTfield forward model, which we also use for the subsequent field-level analysis and for a Fisher forecast of the power- and bispectrum precision. We find significant improvements from the field-level analysis over the power- and bispectrum for a $Λ$CDM cosmology; in particular the primordial fluctuation amplitude can be measured twice as precisely. Precision gains at the field-level are even more pronounced for extended cosmologies with a more flexible dependence between power spectrum shape and structure growth; they reach up to a factor of three in the primordial amplitude and a factor of two in parameters that capture deviations from $Λ$CDM. Our results highlight the potential of field-level analysis for future discoveries. Realizing this potential will require substantial developments on the modeling of observations and observational systematics as well as on the numerical inference techniques.

</details>


### [131] [The WEFT project: I. The emergence of turbulence in a cosmic filament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17205)
*Théo Lebeau,Saleem Zaroubi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本研究通过WEFT项目首次模拟了宇宙细丝中湍流的产生过程，揭示了缺失重子物质（WHIM）在宇宙网状结构中的湍流特性及其观测意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解缺失重子（WHIM）所处的温热星系际介质的湍流与热力学状态，不仅对观测WHIM至关重要，也关乎结构形成中引力坍缩如何将有序流入转化为无序湍流级联的基本问题。

Method: 采用AREPO移动网格代码，对单一宇宙细丝进行从z=63到z=0的宇宙学zoom-in模拟，分辨率达~8 kpc；追踪细丝组装、气体热力学态、吸积激波处涡度生成及湍流增长，并利用高阶速度结构函数与扩展自相似性分析湍流间歇性。

Result: 细丝通过多个原细丝等级并合而非层流吸积形成；涡度在旋转气体流斜向撞击吸积激波时由斜压效应激发；湍流演化从超声速、激波主导态（近似双分形Burgers极限）发展为弱超声速、间歇性强湍流态，z=0时纵向标度指数最接近片状She-Lévêque模型。

Conclusion: 湍流是宇宙细丝弥散气体的内禀可量化属性；该先驱性模拟为WHIM可观测性预测提供了关键湍流输入。

Abstract: Much of the ordinary matter in the present-day Universe still escapes direct detection. These missing baryons are thought to reside in a warm-hot intergalactic medium (WHIM) threading the filaments of the cosmic web, whose turbulent and thermal state remains poorly constrained yet underpins any attempt to observe it. Beyond its observational stakes, how this turbulence emerges, as gravitational collapse converts ordered inflow into a disordered cascade, is a question of structure formation in its own right. We characterise the emergence of turbulence in the diffuse gas of a cosmic filament, from its assembly to the present day, in the first simulation of the Web Evolution in Filament Targeted zoom simulations (WEFT) project. The cosmological zoom-in technique is applied to a single filament, evolved from z=63 to z=0 with the moving-mesh code AREPO, reaching a median gas cell size of ~8 kpc. We trace the filament assembly, the thermodynamic state of its diffuse gas, the generation of vorticity at its accretion shocks, and the growth of turbulent motions, then quantify the intermittency of the cascade through high-order velocity structure functions and their relative scaling exponents from extended self-similarity. The filament assembles by the hierarchical merging of several proto-filaments rather than by laminar accretion. Vorticity is seeded baroclinically where the rotating strands meet their accretion shocks obliquely. The flow evolves from a supersonic, shock-dominated state, statistically close to the bifractal Burgers limit, into a developed, mildly supersonic, intermittent cascade whose longitudinal exponents lie closest to the sheet-like She-Lévêque model at z=0. Turbulence is thus an intrinsic, quantifiable property of the diffuse gas of filaments. This pathfinder run provides the turbulent input for forecasts of WHIM observability.

</details>


### [132] [Running Hubble Constant with the Redshift as a Marker of Evolutionary Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17393)
*Nakia Carlevaro,Giovanni Montani,Maria Giovanna Dainotti*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了哈勃常数随红移变化的物理含义，指出即使在物质与暗能量不直接相互作用的假设下，宇宙动力学仍可用演化暗能量模型描述；基于Ia型超新星数据，发现幂律型和优化拟合型哈勃常数演化均对应类幻影（phantom-like）暗能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释观测到的哈勃常数随红移演化的现象，并探究其背后可能的物理机制。

Method: 在ΛCDM框架下假设物质与暗能量无直接相互作用，结合Pantheon Ia型超新星数据，分别对幂律型和优化拟合型哈勃常数演化进行建模分析。

Result: 两种哈勃常数演化模型均对应于类幻影（phantom-like）的演化暗能量行为。

Conclusion: 即使不引入物质-暗能量相互作用，哈勃常数的红移依赖性也可由演化暗能量模型自然刻画，且该暗能量具有phantom-like性质。

Abstract: We discuss the interpretation of an observed running Hubble constant with the redshift, in terms of the possible underlying physical scenario. Assuming, like in the $Λ$CDM model, that the matter and dark energy components do not directly interact, we arrive at the conclusion that the physical content of the cosmological dynamics can always be represented with an evolutionary dark energy paradigm. We discuss both the case of the power-law running Hubble constant, and that of an optimized fitting model, both studied with the binned Pantheon Sample of the Type Ia Supernovae data. These two cases turn out to be associated with a phantom-like evolutionary dark energy.

</details>


### [133] [The Equivalence Principle in the Dark Sector in light of DESI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17423)
*Andrea Clini,Pierre Zhang,Emanuele Castorina,Maria Archidiacono*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出暗物质粒子的惯性质量与引力质量不相等，以此解释DESI星系分布观测与CMB推断的ΛCDM模型在低红移处的轻微张力；拟合结果表明暗物质中可能存在强度约为引力0.5%的新长程力，显著性达2.5–3σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释DESI观测到的星系分布与基于CMB外推的ΛCDM模型在低红移处存在的轻微张力。

Method: 引入暗物质惯性质量与引力质量不等的理论框架，拟合CMB数据联合DESI DR1（BAO+Full Shape）或DR2（BAO）数据，并分析新暗力参数β的统计显著性。

Result: 最佳拟合显示存在强度β ∼ 0.5%（相对于引力）的暗区长程力，在2.5σ水平上支持该信号；结合FS DR1与BAO DR2的初步分析将证据提升至∼3σ（忽略尚未可用的交叉相关）。

Conclusion: 暗物质中可能存在新长程相互作用，其效应可通过惯性-引力质量分离建模，为解决宇宙学尺度上的观测张力提供一种有统计支持的新物理方案。

Abstract: Recent measurements of the distribution of galaxies by the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) are in mild tension with a standard $Λ$-Cold Dark Matter cosmological model inferred from the anisotropies of the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) when extrapolated to low redshifts. Several solutions to explain this discrepancy in terms of new physics have been put forward, notably from new dynamics in a complex Dark Sector. Here we report on a resolution to this discrepancy that postulates that the inertial and gravitational masses of dark matter particles are not the same. Our best fit to CMB data combined either with Baryon Acoustic Oscillation (BAO) and Full Shape (FS) data from DESI DR1 (Data Release 1), or with BAO DR2 data alone, suggests the presence of a new long-range force in the Dark Sector with strength of $β\sim 0.5\% $ relative to gravity, at the $\sim 2.5σ$ level. A preliminary analysis combining FS DR1 and BAO DR2 data confirms the statistical power of DR2 and raises the evidence for dark forces to $\sim 3σ$, albeit ignoring the not yet available cross-correlation between the two datasets.

</details>


### [134] [$B$-sure. Part II. Scattering transforms as robustness test for tensor-to-scalar ratio detection from CMB observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17531)
*Claudio Ranucci,Sébastien Pierre,Léo Vacher,Alessandro Carones,Nicoletta Krachmalnicoff,Erwan Allys,Jean-Marc Delouis,Paolo Campeti,Giuseppe Puglisi,Carlo Baccigalupi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用散射变换（ST）检测CMB B模中前景残差非高斯性以验证原初B模探测鲁棒性的新方法，能在约90%的模拟中识别出r偏差≥10⁻³，达到LiteBIRD实验精度目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系前景是测量原初B模的主要污染源，即使经过成分分离，残差仍可能偏倚张量标量比r的估计，导致假阳性探测，亟需鲁棒性验证方法。

Method: 采用散射变换（ST）作为对前景残差非高斯性及其与前景示踪图相关性敏感的统计量，在不同复杂度的真实天幕模拟上进行方法表征与验证。

Result: 该方法在约90%的模拟中可识别出r偏差≥10⁻³；当偏差低于实验r灵敏度时测试通过，不发出警告。

Conclusion: 该测试为未来宇宙学参数测量（尤其是r）抵御前景污染提供了重要的统计验证工具，是迈向可靠原初引力波探测的关键一步。

Abstract: Galactic foregrounds represent a major contamination to the measurement of primordial $B$-modes from observations of the Cosmic Microwave Background polarisation. Even after the application of component separation algorithms, foreground residuals may potentially still bias the estimate of the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$, causing a false detection. In this work, we present the methodology of a robustness test for the validation of an eventual detection of primordial $B$-modes, as obtained by a future, LiteBIRD-like satellite experiment. The goal of the test is to identify the foreground residuals contamination by looking for non-Gaussian properties in the CMB $B$-modes map, recovered through blind component separation algorithms. We adopt scattering transforms (ST) as our summary statistics sensitive to the non-Gaussian features of foreground residuals and to their correlation with foregrounds tracer maps. We characterise and validate the methodology on realistic sky simulations with different levels of foregrounds complexity. The proposed test is able to identify a bias on the tensor-to-scalar ratio of $\gtrsim 10^{-3}$ in $\sim 90\%$ of our simulations, with this bias value being of the same order of the accuracy targeted by LiteBIRD. Additionally, for our particular experimental configuration, the test is passed when the bias is lower than the sensitivity on the $r$ parameter, and no warning is raised. These results provide an important step forward in the development of statistical tools for validating future measurement of cosmological parameters, against foregrounds contamination.

</details>
